### [1] [DINOv3-MIL: Per-Kidney Multi-Label Tumour and Cyst Detection from Foundation-Model Patch Tokens on KiTS23](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22687)
*Vishalakshi M,Sahil Sharma,Pramod Kumar P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种聚合器（CLS线性探针、注意力MIL、原型头）在冻结DINOv3 ViT-H/16特征上对肾脏肿瘤/囊肿检测的性能，发现注意力MIL在AUROC上最优但原型头在囊肿检测上失效，揭示了可解释性与性能之间的权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学影像（肾脏肿瘤/囊肿检测），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测轻量化、在轨部署等核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学影像分类/检测（肾肿瘤和囊肿检测）；应用场景：医学诊断；传感器类型：CT/MRI等医学影像（摘要未明确说明）；目标类别：肾脏肿瘤（tumour）和囊肿（cyst）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基础模型：DINOv3 ViT-H/16（冻结特征）；聚合器：CLS-token linear probe、gated attention multiple instance learning (MIL)、prototype head (ProtoViT)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。ViT-H/16模型本身参数量巨大，不适合星上部署，但本文未讨论轻量化或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KiTS23（966个肾脏，n=97测试）；评价指标：AUROC（含95%置信区间）。

Experiments: 注意力MIL在肿瘤检测上AUROC 0.74（0.64-0.83），囊肿检测上AUROC 0.80（0.70-0.88），注意力在标注病灶内富集7.5-9.8倍；原型头在囊肿检测上AUROC仅0.51，表现失效。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；3. 使用ViT-H/16这种超大模型，计算开销极高；4. 仅针对医学影像场景，方法迁移到遥感场景需大量验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是：注意力MIL作为聚合器处理大量patch token的思路，在遥感大场景下处理超大尺寸遥感图像时可能有启发，但本文未涉及遥感场景和轻量化，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Foundation vision models trained on natural images transfer to medical tasks without domain pre-training, but volumetric classification requires aggregating tens of thousands of patch tokens per study, and the aggregator constrains how the resulting model can be interpreted. We compare three aggregators on identical frozen DINOv3 ViT-H/16+ features for renal tumour/cyst detection on KiTS23 (966 kidneys; n=97 test): a CLS-token linear probe, gated attention multiple instance learning (MIL) over 55,296 patch tokens, and a prototype head following ProtoViT. Attention MIL achieves the highest AUROC for tumour (0.74, 95% CI 0.64-0.83) and cyst (0.80, 0.70-0.88), with attention enriched 7.5-9.8x over chance within annotated lesions. The prototype head does not transfer to cyst detection (AUROC 0.51), exposing an interpretability-performance trade-off at this token scale.

</details>


### [2] [MegaSlide-DiT: Memory-Centric Adaptation and Deformable Local Attention for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22696)
*Jiacheng Liu,Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MegaSlide-DiT系统，通过将模型状态驻留于主机内存、按需流式传输到GPU，并结合3D可变形滑动注意力机制，实现在单张H200 GPU上适配105B参数的视频扩散模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大规模视频扩散模型（DiT）在单GPU工作站上的适配问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。其核心是系统级的内存管理和注意力机制优化，属于生成式AI和大模型推理系统方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大规模视频扩散模型（DiT）的适配/微调；应用场景：单GPU工作站上的大模型部署；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Diffusion Transformer (DiT)架构，提出3D Deformable Slide Attention (3D-DSA)替代全局自注意力，将计算和内存复杂度从二次降为线性。

Lightweight method: 不涉及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。采用系统级优化：将模型权重、优化器状态驻留于主机内存（1.5TB RAM），仅将当前需要的分片流式传输到GPU，属于内存卸载（memory offloading）策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等星上部署关键指标。仅提及在单张H200 GPU + 1.5TB主机RAM上运行105B参数模型，但这与星上/边缘部署的硬件约束（有限内存、低功耗、实时性）完全不符。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法，仅提及报告详细的内存记录、执行轨迹和评估结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测方法；3. 不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）；4. 系统级内存卸载策略依赖大容量主机RAM（1.5TB），不适合星上资源受限场景；5. 未讨论推理速度、实时性等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：3D-DSA中"将全局注意力替换为局部滑动注意力以降低复杂度"的思路，在遥感大图上的目标检测中可类比用于降低高分辨率遥感图像上的自注意力计算量。但该论文本身不涉及检测任务，且其系统级内存卸载策略对星上部署不适用。

Abstract: High-resolution video diffusion models built on Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) deliver strong fidelity but quickly exhaust the memory budget of a single workstation. A 100 billion-plus parameter DiT easily requires over a terabyte of persistent state, while naive spatiotemporal self-attention grows quadratically in sequence length. These two walls -- parameter memory and activation memory -- prevent researchers from adapting massive generative models without large GPU clusters. We revisit this problem from a systems perspective and introduce MegaSlide-DiT, a prototype that demonstrates how a pre-trained 105B DiT can be adapted on a single H200 GPU with 1.5 TB of host RAM. Our key insight is that the GPU need not own the model state: all persistent weights, master weights and optimizer moments remain in host memory, while only transient shards are streamed to the GPU on demand. Simultaneously, we replace quadratic global attention with 3D Deformable Slide Attention (3D-DSA), a motion-adaptive local attention operator that reduces both memory and computational complexity to linear in the sequence length. We report detailed memory accounting, execution traces and evaluation results to substantiate our design. MegaSlide-DiT does not claim to train a 105B model from scratch on a single GPU, nor does it magically solve bandwidth limits; rather, it offers a pragmatic path for full-parameter adaptation of massive video diffusion models on high-end workstations.

</details>


### [3] [FogDrive: A Multi-Modal Synthetic Driving Dataset for Perception under Graded Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22698)
*Vansh Panwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个多模态自动驾驶雾天数据集FogDrive，包含同步的RGB、深度、语义分割、LiDAR和雷达数据，用于评估"去雾-检测"流水线下的鲁棒感知性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景下的恶劣天气（雾天）多模态感知，不涉及遥感卫星、在轨智能、星上部署或遥感目标检测。虽然涉及目标检测（3D/2D检测），但场景是自动驾驶而非遥感/卫星/航空图像，且完全不涉及模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态3D目标检测与2D图像恢复；应用场景：自动驾驶（adverse weather）；传感器类型：RGB相机、深度相机、语义分割相机、LiDAR、语义LiDAR、前向雷达；目标类别：车辆等自动驾驶相关目标。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: 摘要提及的基线架构：TransFusion、BEVFusion（3D多模态融合）、YOLOv8-m（2D检测）。摘要未说明具体模型结构设计细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FogDrive（自建，基于CARLA模拟器），660个场景，约133k帧，50:50昼夜比，4个同步相机（RGB、深度、语义分割）、LiDAR+语义LiDAR、前向雷达。雾浓度分三级（160m、100m、50m能见度）。评价指标：2D/3D检测指标（摘要未具体说明mAP等）、语义分割质量审计（95.1% precision，>99% recall for vehicles within 40m）、图像质量指标（PSNR、SSIM）。

Experiments: 基线实验：TransFusion、BEVFusion（3D多模态融合）、YOLOv8-m（2D检测）。关键发现：1）训练时混合多密度雾可收紧3D边界框几何而不增加数据规模；2）2D流水线中图像质量指标（PSNR、SSIM）不能很好地预测下游检测性能。

Limitations: 1）基于模拟器（CARLA），存在sim-to-real gap；2）仅针对雾天，未覆盖雨、雪等其他恶劣天气；3）场景为自动驾驶，与遥感卫星场景差异巨大；4）未涉及任何轻量化或边缘部署讨论。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可参考的点：1）多密度数据混合训练策略（mixing multi-density fog）对检测性能提升的思路，可类比遥感图像中多尺度/多条件数据增强；2）图像质量指标（PSNR/SSIM）与下游检测性能脱钩的发现，提示在遥感场景中不应仅依赖图像质量指标评估模型。但整体与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化方向关联度低。

Abstract: Perception under adverse weather remains a critical bottleneck for reliable autonomous driving, yet existing benchmarks lack the systematic multi-modal alignments needed to evaluate robust sensor fusion. Real-world weather datasets suffer from uncontrolled collection and single-level, uncalibrated conditions, while synthetic alternatives either target camera-only restoration or lack the paired clean-and-foggy structure needed to benchmark "defog-then-detect" pipelines. We present FogDrive, a rigorously calibrated, multi-modal autonomous-driving dataset bridging data-centric engineering and robust machine learning. Built with the CARLA simulator, FogDrive contains 660 scenes (~133k fully annotated frames, 50:50 day/night) across four synchronized cameras (RGB, depth, semantic segmentation), a LiDAR and semantic-LiDAR pair, and front radar. Physically consistent fog is modeled independently on camera channels (Koschmieder model) and LiDAR channels (Beer-Lambert law) at three calibrated visibility densities (160m, 100m, 50m). Every scene ships in four matched variants (clean plus three graded fog levels) with cross-calibrated 2D and 3D bounding boxes. A semantic-segmentation-based quality audit over 8k images validates annotations at 95.1% precision and over 99% recall for vehicles within 40m. We establish baseline benchmarks with state-of-the-art architectures (TransFusion, BEVFusion, YOLOv8-m) across two paradigms: 3D multi-modal fusion and 2D image restoration. These yield critical data-centric insights: mixing multi-density fog during training tightens 3D bounding-box geometry without added data-scaling cost, while in 2D pipelines image-quality metrics (PSNR, SSIM) prove poor predictors of downstream detection performance. FogDrive will be fully open-sourced alongside our data-generation framework to accelerate robust, multi-modal research.

</details>


### [4] [MIME: Multimodal Interactive Motion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22702)
*Addison Zucek,Prerit Gupta,Kamila Kuatova,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIME，首个面向双人交互运动的多模态编码器，用于文本-运动检索和下游运动生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是人体运动（human motion）的文本-运动多模态表示学习，面向动画、AR/VR和具身AI场景，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：文本-运动检索（text-motion retrieval）和运动生成；应用场景：动画、AR/VR、具身AI；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：双人交互运动（two-person interactive motion）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: MIME（Multimodal Interactive Motion Encoder），基于流的共注意力机制（stream-based co-attention），包含显式交互特征（explicit interaction features）和课程对比训练（curriculum-based contrastive training）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Inter-X（文本-运动检索）、InterHuman（下游运动生成评估）；评价指标：R@1（text-to-motion retrieval）、FID（Fréchet Inception Distance，运动生成质量）、语义对齐指标（semantic alignment metrics）。

Experiments: 在Inter-X文本-运动检索任务上，MIME在2000样本库中text-to-motion R@1相对提升12.8%，优于early fusion和late fusion基线。在InterHuman数据集上，作为冻结辅助先验（frozen auxiliary prior）集成到TIMotion和InterMask中，改善了语义对齐指标，TIMotion的FID保持可比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。摘要未说明模型计算开销、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，多模态交互表示中的共注意力机制（co-attention）思路在遥感多模态融合（如光学-SAR融合检测）中可能有理论上的启发，但论文本身未涉及遥感场景，且无轻量化设计，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Text-motion representation learning has advanced rapidly, with growing interest in multi person interactions for animation, AR/VR, and embodied AI. These settings require representations that align language with both individual actor dynamics and the relationships between actors. We introduce the Multimodal Interactive Motion Encoder (MIME), which, to our knowledge, represents the first dedicated multimodal encoder designed specifically for two person interactive motion. MIME captures individual and shared structure using stream based co-attention with explicit interaction features and curriculum based contrastive training. On Inter-X text-motion retrieval, MIME consistently outperforms early and late fusion baselines across gallery sizes, achieving a 12.8% relative improvement in text-to-motion R@1 at a 2,000-sample gallery. We further evaluate MIME as a frozen auxiliary prior within TIMotion and InterMask on the unseen InterHuman dataset. MIME improves semantic alignment metrics while maintaining comparable FID in TIMotion. These results show that interaction aware multimodal encoding improves multi person motion retrieval and transfers across datasets to support downstream motion generation.

</details>


### [5] [Histopathological Spectrum-Guided Prostate Stratification via Segmentation-Assisted Diagnostic Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22703)
*Leyang Li,Lihua Chen,Huangang Hu,Tianhang Hao,Hao Cheng,Xin Zhang,Qianru Sun,Bingxu Lu,Wenlong Yu,Feng Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语言引导分割辅助的诊断Transformer模型（LSDT），用于前列腺癌多参数MRI的细粒度四分类，构建了PCa-HSD数据集，通过零样本分割提供解剖先验并进行多模态切片融合提升分类精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学影像（前列腺癌MRI诊断），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

<details>

Model architecture: LSDT（Language-guided Segmentation-assisted Diagnostic Transformer），结合零样本分割提供解剖先验，进行多模态切片融合分类。具体结构细节摘要未充分说明。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PCa-HSD（Prostate Cancer Histopathology Spectrum Dataset），344名患者队列；评价指标：Accuracy（最佳0.633）、JointRecall（最佳0.768），五折交叉验证。

Experiments: 在344名患者队列上进行五折交叉验证，LSDT在不同骨干网络上均提升精度，最佳平均准确率0.633，JointRecall 0.768。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 医学影像领域，数据规模较小（344名患者），精度偏低（0.633）；3. 未讨论模型效率、部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：1）语言引导的分割辅助方法（zero-shot segmentation提供先验）可类比遥感中利用文本/语义先验辅助检测；2）多模态融合策略（多参数MRI切片融合）可类比遥感中多光谱/高光谱波段融合。但整体方向差异大，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Prostate cancer diagnosis with multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) is commonly based on PI-RADS assessment or binary classification, which suffer from subjectivity and fail to capture clinically relevant pathological heterogeneity. To address this limitation, we construct a Prostate Cancer Histopathology Spectrum Dataset (PCa-HSD) and formulate a clinically meaningful four-class classification task, addressing the underrepresentation of benign lesions that are easily confounded with prostate cancer in existing datasets. We propose Language-guided Segmentation-assisted Diagnostic Transformer model (LSDT), which leverages zero-shot segmentation to provide anatomical priors and performs effective multi-modal slice fusion for classification. Our proposed method consistently improves accuracy across backbones, achieving the best average accuracy of 0.633 and JointRecall of 0.768 in five-fold cross-validation on a cohort of 344 patients. These results demonstrate that integrating pathology supervision and anatomical priors significantly enhances fine-grained prostate MRI classification and provides a more clinically relevant paradigm for risk stratification. Code will be made publicly available in a future revision.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：等离子体光强二维空间分布预测（视频帧输入→光强分布图输出）；场景：核聚变装置（EAST托卡马克）可见光相机观测；传感器：可见光相机；目标类别：无（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: Delta-InvFormer，基于差分Transformer（differential Transformer）的骨干网络，包含空间差分自注意力（spatial differential self-attention）和时间差分自注意力（temporal differential self-attention），用于捕获视频帧间的等离子体动态并抑制噪声。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"显著加速传统方法"，但未给出具体加速比或硬件平台信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EAST（Experimental Advanced Superconducting Tokamak）真实实验数据；评价指标：重建精度（reconstruction accuracy），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在EAST真实数据上验证，模型相比传统方法显著加速且达到竞争性重建精度。摘要未给出与其他深度方法的对比结果或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未提供任何轻量化指标（参数量、FLOPs等）；3. 未说明具体加速比和硬件平台；4. 未说明具体评价指标名称；5. 应用场景高度专一（核聚变等离子体），方法迁移到遥感场景的可行性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。差分自注意力机制（spatial/temporal differential self-attention）作为一种去噪特征提取思路，理论上可迁移到遥感视频目标检测中抑制时序噪声，但论文未涉及目标检测和轻量化，且场景差异巨大。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Nuclear fusion has made significant progress in recent years and is expected to become one of the most important pathways to addressing global energy challenges. This paper focuses on observing plasma using visible-light cameras, analyzing its spatio-temporal motion cues, and predicting the two-dimensional spatial distribution of light intensity, aiming to provide a foundational basis for future scientific experiments using deep neural networks. Specifically, we propose Delta-InvFormer, a novel backbone network centered on a differential Transformer. The key insight is that by taking consecutive video frames as input, we can better capture the dynamics of the plasma. Moreover, spatial and temporal differential self-attention effectively mitigates interference from noisy signals, ensuring high-quality feature extraction. These features are then fused into a compact and informative representation, which is fed into a decoder network to predict the distribution. Based on real experimental data collected from the Experimental Advanced Superconducting Tokamak (EAST) large-scale scientific facility, our results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly accelerates traditional methods for distribution prediction but also achieves competitive reconstruction accuracy. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion

</details>


### [7] [EditCLEVR: A Paired-Scene Intervention Benchmark for Compositional Faithfulness of Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22705)
*Anuraag Gadehothur Karnam,Tarunesh Sathish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EditCLEVR基准，用于评估面向对象表示在受控语义编辑下的行为正确性，引入SGIA和Delta-SGIA指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：object-centric representation evaluation（面向对象表示评估）；场景：合成CLEVR场景（非遥感场景）；传感器类型：合成渲染图像；目标类别：CLEVR几何体（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 评估的模型包括：ground-truth-mask backbones、learned-slot models、SAM 2 + frozen-ViT models、mask-feature hybrid。不涉及遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EditCLEVR（基于CLEVR合成的配对场景干预基准）；指标：SGIA (Scene-Graph Intervention Accuracy)、Delta-SGIA、probe-free diagnostics、probe-decoded semantic faithfulness metrics。

Experiments: 在ground-truth-mask backbones、learned-slot models、SAM 2 + frozen-ViT models、mask-feature hybrid上进行基线评估，发现CoGenT-OOD-core退化在真实实例掩码下仍然存在，掩码来源只能解释部分性能，仅靠局部性或稳定性会高估语义忠实度。

Limitations: 1. 完全基于合成CLEVR场景，与真实遥感图像分布差异巨大；2. 不涉及遥感目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 评估的是面向对象表示学习，而非检测模型的精度或效率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，直接借鉴价值有限。但SGIA这类语义编辑正确性评估思路，如果未来要评估遥感检测模型在属性变化（如目标朝向、尺寸变化）下的鲁棒性，可参考其干预式评估框架。不过这与轻量化课题的核心目标（模型压缩、加速、星上部署）关联甚微。

Abstract: Object-centric learning aims to represent scenes as objects whose properties can be reused in new combinations. Existing evaluations usually score segmentation, single-image factor prediction, or downstream accuracy, but these tests do not directly ask whether a per-object representation behaves correctly under a controlled semantic edit. We introduce EditCLEVR, a paired-scene intervention benchmark in which each example contains a before/after pair of CLEVR-style renders with the same object indices and scene layout, and either exactly one known attribute change on one known object or a no-edit re-render for drift measurement. The protocol includes probe-free diagnostics for representation-change localization and stability, together with probe-decoded semantic faithfulness metrics that test whether the predicted scene change matches the intended intervention across in-distribution and compositional out-of-distribution (OOD) suites, allowing code-space movement and decoded object-attribute correctness to be evaluated separately. We introduce the semantic metric Scene-Graph Intervention Accuracy (SGIA), which requires the full after-scene prediction to be correct and the only predicted before-to-after semantic change to be the intended object-factor edit. We also establish Delta-SGIA as a companion diagnostic that checks the single-site change pattern without requiring the full after-scene graph to be correct. Baseline evaluations on ground-truth-mask backbones, learned-slot models, SAM 2 + frozen-ViT models, and one mask-feature hybrid indicate that CoGenT-OOD-core degradation can persist under ground-truth instance masks, that mask source accounts for part but not all of native performance, and that locality or stability alone can overstate semantic faithfulness. Code is available at https://github.com/torux-bughunter/EditCLEVR.

</details>


### [8] [LowAux-RDNet: Low-Pass Residual Supervision with Scene-Balanced Real-World Training for Single-Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22707)
*Jizhong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RDNet的显式分解流水线，通过训练阶段的低通反射辅助目标（LowAux）和场景平衡的真实数据对（RRW）来提升单幅图像反射去除性能，并构建了统一的评估基准。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是单幅图像反射去除（single-image reflection removal），属于底层视觉/图像恢复任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：单幅图像反射去除（single-image reflection removal）；场景：透过玻璃拍摄的日常照片；传感器类型：普通RGB相机；目标类别：无检测目标，仅涉及图像层分解（透射层与反射层）。

Model architecture: 基于RDNet的显式分解流水线（explicit decomposition pipeline），引入LowAux（低通反射辅助目标）作为训练阶段的辅助监督。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文聚焦于图像恢复质量而非推理效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CEILNet, Real20, Postcard, Objects, Wild（共5个反射去除标准数据集），以及RRW场景平衡真实数据对；评价指标：PSNR, SSIM, NCC, LMSE；建立了统一的公共评估基准以避免预处理和度量代码差异。

Experiments: 五数据集宏平均：27.546 dB PSNR, 0.9220 SSIM, 0.9751 NCC, 0.004760 LMSE，在对比的公开checkpoint和内部变体中取得最高宏平均PSNR、SSIM、NCC和最低LMSE。Postcard数据集中清晰语义反射仍具挑战性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论模型效率、参数量、推理速度；3. 对强语义反射（如Postcard数据集）处理效果有限；4. 仅在普通RGB图像上验证，未涉及遥感多光谱/高光谱场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1. 训练阶段的辅助损失设计思路（LowAux作为低频约束）可类比于遥感目标检测中引入频域先验或背景抑制损失；2. 统一评估基准的构建思路可迁移到遥感在轨检测模型的标准化评测。但整体与课题方向差距较大。

Abstract: Single-image reflection removal aims to recover a clean transmission layer from one image captured through glass. We study an explicit decomposition pipeline built on RDNet and introduce LowAux, a training-only low-pass reflection auxiliary objective. The original residual target remains the main reflection supervision, while symmetrically filtered prediction and target provide a stable low-frequency constraint. We further incorporate scene-balanced real pairs from RRW to broaden real-scene coverage and improve cross-dataset generalization. To avoid evaluation discrepancies caused by model-specific resizing, padding, output quantization, and metric code, we build a unified public benchmark over CEILNet, Real20, Postcard, Objects, and Wild. Under the same evaluator, the proposed system obtains a five-dataset macro average of 27.546 dB PSNR, 0.9220 SSIM, 0.9751 NCC, and 0.004760 LMSE, achieving the highest macro-average PSNR, SSIM, and NCC and the lowest LMSE among the compared public checkpoints and internal variants. Per-dataset and qualitative analyses show that the main benefit is a more balanced performance across diverse reflection distributions, while clear semantic reflections in Postcard remain challenging.

</details>


### [9] [StepX-Edge: An On-Device UI Vision-Language Model via Architecture-Training-Deployment Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22708)
*Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Jineng Han,Wentao Qiu,Zhenhua Ge,Liujian Tang,Fanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StepX-Edge，一个0.9B参数的端侧UI视觉语言模型，通过架构-训练-部署三层协同设计，在移动NPU上实现高精度UI理解与高效推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于移动端UI界面理解（OCR、屏幕理解、VQA、元素定位），属于端侧VLM部署，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅量化部署策略有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：UI理解（OCR、屏幕理解、视觉问答、元素定位）；应用场景：移动端设备上的UI交互；传感器类型：屏幕截图（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：UI元素（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 0.9B参数VLM，包含UI-aware Layered Visual Encoding (ULVE)和Progressive Dimensionality Projection (PDP) connector，使用标准full attention以保证与移动NPU算子兼容。

Lightweight method: 模块级差异化两阶段PTQ-to-QAT量化方案（W4A16+KV8量化），后量化精度损失控制在1%以内。模型参数量0.9B，属于轻量级VLM设计。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：Snapdragon 8 Gen5移动芯片（NPU）；量化后指标：TTFT约0.84s，解码速度98 tok/s，峰值内存1.4 GB。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、能耗等具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ScreenQA（F1=88.76）、Chinese OCRBench v2（57.25）、RefCOCO（92.0%）、OCRBench v1（831）；评价指标：F1、准确率等。

Experiments: 在<=1B模型中取得最强UI理解性能，超越所有2B-2.3B基线模型；量化后精度损失<1%；在Snapdragon 8 Gen5上稳定运行。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，方法不可直接迁移；2. 0.9B参数对星上部署仍偏大；3. 仅验证了移动NPU，未涉及FPGA或星上硬件；4. 未报告FLOPs和能耗数据。

Ideas for my research: 1. PTQ-to-QAT两阶段量化策略可借鉴用于遥感模型星上量化部署；2. 模块级差异化量化（不同模块不同量化位宽）思路可用于遥感检测模型；3. 架构-训练-部署三层协同设计方法论可迁移；4. 标准full attention保证NPU兼容性的思路对星上NPU部署有参考价值。

Abstract: Deploying a vision-language model with full UI understanding on end devices has long been trapped between accuracy and efficiency: on one side is the accuracy bar for OCR, screen understanding, visual question answering, and element grounding; on the other is the strict compute, memory, and power budget of mobile chips. Existing work either trades one for the other, or stops at simulation without real-device validation. We present StepX-Edge, a 0.9B-parameter on-device UI vision-language model that resolves this tension through three-layer co-design of architecture, training, and deployment. Architecturally, UI-aware Layered Visual Encoding (ULVE) and a Progressive Dimensionality Projection (PDP) connector target the extreme aspect ratios and fine-grained perception of screens, while standard full attention throughout ensures native compatibility with mainstream mobile NPU operators. For training, the five-stage StepX-Curriculum framework is designed around our observation of mutual-promotion effects among UI subtasks, so that all four capabilities grow synergistically under a tight parameter budget rather than interfering. For deployment, a module-wise differentiated two-stage PTQ-to-QAT quantization scheme keeps the post-quantization accuracy loss within 1%. StepX-Edge achieves the strongest overall UI understanding among <=1B models, surpassing all 2B-2.3B baselines on ScreenQA (88.76 F1) and Chinese OCRBench v2 (57.25), and matching 1.3B-2.3B general VLMs on RefCOCO (92.0%) and OCRBench v1 (831) with far fewer parameters. After W4A16+KV8 quantization, the model runs stably on Snapdragon 8 Gen5 devices with ~0.84 s TTFT, 98 tok/s decode, and 1.4 GB peak memory. We will open-source the training data, the full training recipe, and the quantization deployment pipeline.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出GeoMVC多模态交互与多视角共识方法，用于社交媒体迷因(meme)中的厌女内容检测，在ICMI 2026 CC-MMD挑战赛中取得多语言分区前列排名。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态厌女内容检测（misogyny detection in memes）；场景：社交媒体内容审核；传感器/数据：图像+文本（OCR、code-mixed transliteration）；目标类别：厌女/非厌女二分类。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: GeoMVC：包含Geometric Interaction Layer（Hadamard积+余弦相似度实现跨模态对齐）和Multi-View Consensus策略（聚合raw、length-filtered、English-translated三种文本视图的预测）。视觉和文本编码器为冻结（frozen）的预训练模型。摘要未说明具体backbone。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该任务场景（社交媒体内容审核）与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CC-MMD Grand Challenge（ICMI 2026），包含Malayalam、Chinese、Tamil三个语言分区；评价指标：Macro F1；结果：Malayalam分区Macro F1=0.892（Rank 2），Chinese分区Macro F1=0.895（Rank 3），Tamil分区Macro F1=0.521（Rank 5）。

Experiments: 在CC-MMD挑战赛三个语言分区上取得排名，并进行了开发集上的错误分析（error analysis），指出localized transliteration和code-mixed sarcasm是开放挑战。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未提供模型效率指标；3. Tamil分区Macro F1仅0.521，性能有限；4. 方法依赖冻结的预训练视觉/文本编码器，未讨论微调或端到端训练。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，Geometric Interaction Layer中的Hadamard积+余弦相似度跨模态对齐思路，或许可启发遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）特征融合中的轻量交互设计，但需大幅改造且效率未知。

Abstract: The proliferation of internet memes has introduced new complexities to automated content moderation, particularly in detecting misogyny. Memes often rely on a semantic clash between visual and textual modalities, where hateful intent is implicit and culturally grounded. This paper presents GeoMVC (Geometric Interaction and Multi-View Consensus), developed for the CC-MMD Grand Challenge at ICMI 2026. To address the limitations of static feature concatenation, a Geometric Interaction Layer is proposed that models cross-modal alignment via Hadamard products and cosine similarity between frozen visual and textual embeddings. We further mitigate distribution shifts caused by noisy OCR and code-mixed transliteration through a Multi-View Consensus strategy, aggregating predictions across raw, length-filtered, and English-translated text views. The system achieved Rank 2 in the Malayalam partition (Macro F1: 0.892) and Rank 3 in the Chinese partition (Macro F1: 0.895) on Task A, while securing Rank 5 in the Tamil partition (Macro F1: 0.521). A detailed error analysis on the development partition highlights open challenges in modeling localized transliteration and code-mixed sarcasm across Dravidian and Chinese cultural contexts.

</details>

Experiments: scMIR在细胞分类、聚类、表型推断、批次效应校正等任务上优于现有通用模型和任务导向方法，且无需任务特定微调即展现出强泛化能力。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无可借鉴性；2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度等效率指标；3. 仅针对光学显微镜图像，领域迁移性差。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。视觉-语言基础模型的方法论（自监督+跨模态对齐）在遥感领域有类似工作（如遥感CLIP），但本文面向细胞图像，领域差异过大，不建议参考。

Abstract: Single-cell light microscopy images have become an important data source for characterizing cell phenotypes, but their complexity and heterogeneity pose challenges to high-throughput automated analysis. Existing representation learning methods mostly rely on task-oriented modeling, which is limited by specific datasets and predefined tasks, making them difficult to generalize across different cell types and microscopy modalities, and experimental conditions. Although general-purpose methods have improved the generalization ability of image representation in recent years, their limited utilization of experimental background and biological context information still poses challenges in complex phenotypic analysis. Here, we propose scMIR, a vision-language foundation model for single-cell light microscopy image representation. By synergistically combining self-supervised image reconstruction with text-guided cross-modal alignment, scMIR can simultaneously encode morphological and biological semantic information in a unified representation space. scMIR is pre-trained on 207,957 image-text pairs, covering various cell types, microscopy modalities, and perturbation conditions. scMIR outperforms existing general models and task-oriented methods as systematically evaluated on various complex tasks using 16 benchmark datasets, including cell classification, clustering, phenotype inference, and batch effect correction tasks. Furthermore, scMIR shows a strong generalization ability across various tasks without requiring task-specific fine-tuning. With its unique advantages, we envision scMIR may promote the standardization and automation of high-throughput phenotyping workflows through supporting various downstream analysis tasks.

</details>


### [12] [Real-Time Semantic Segmentation with Optimized RetinaNet Architectures for Embedded Automotive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22714)
*Sai Sidharth D*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从RetinaNet检测框架衍生的语义分割架构Opt-RetinaSeg，结合结构化剪枝、INT8量化和知识蒸馏三阶段优化，在嵌入式汽车平台上实现实时语义分割。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及目标检测框架(RetinaNet)的衍生、轻量化技术(剪枝+量化+蒸馏)和嵌入式部署(Jetson Xavier NX/Qualcomm SoC)，这些方法对遥感在轨轻量化有借鉴意义。但应用场景是自动驾驶/ADAS(道路场景)，传感器为车载摄像头，数据集为Cityscapes/BDD100K，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或航空影像，属于泛视觉嵌入式部署方向。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。轻量化方法论(剪枝+量化+蒸馏三阶段)有借鉴价值，但场景完全不涉及遥感/卫星，且任务是语义分割而非目标检测。如果课题聚焦于轻量化技术组合策略，可快速浏览其优化流水线设计；如果课题聚焦遥感特定问题(旋转框、小目标、多光谱等)，则优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语义分割(从RetinaNet检测框架衍生)；应用场景：ADAS/自动驾驶道路场景感知；传感器类型：车载摄像头(摘要未明确说明传感器类型，但从数据集Cityscapes/BDD100K推断为车载RGB相机)；目标类别：道路场景语义类别(行人、车辆、道路等，摘要未列出具体类别)。

Model architecture: 基础框架：RetinaNet检测框架衍生的语义分割架构Opt-RetinaSeg；Backbone：混合轻量特征提取器(替换标准ResNet-50)；Neck：重构FPN以减少冗余多尺度计算；Head：紧凑分割头，引入focal-loss启发的类别平衡策略处理前景-背景不平衡。

Lightweight method: 三阶段优化流水线：(1) 结构化通道剪枝(structured channel pruning)；(2) 训练后INT8量化(post-training INT8 quantization)；(3) 知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation from high-capacity teacher network)。摘要未涉及NAS或轻量网络架构搜索。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX 和 Qualcomm QCS610 汽车级SoC；推理速度：70.4 FPS；加速比：7.4x(相对于ResNet-50基线)；模型尺寸缩减：4x(相对于ResNet-50基线)；精度损失：<3% mIoU退化；参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗：摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Cityscapes、BDD100K(均为自动驾驶道路场景数据集，非遥感数据集)；评价指标：mIoU(73.9%)、FPS(70.4)；摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs等具体数值。

Experiments: 主要结果：73.9% mIoU @ 70.4 FPS；对比基线：ResNet-50 baseline；消融实验：摘要未说明是否进行了消融实验；对比方法：摘要未列出其他对比方法。

Limitations: (1) 场景为自动驾驶而非遥感/卫星，传感器模态和任务目标差异大；(2) 语义分割任务而非目标检测，虽然从RetinaNet衍生但输出形式不同；(3) 未说明参数量、FLOPs等关键轻量化指标的具体数值；(4) 未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有挑战；(5) 未讨论星上部署特有的辐射/存储/通信约束。

Ideas for my research: (1) 三阶段优化流水线(剪枝→量化→蒸馏)的流程设计可直接迁移到遥感在轨检测模型轻量化中；(2) 从检测框架(RetinaNet)衍生为其他任务(分割)的思路，可启发将遥感检测模型适配为多任务在轨处理；(3) 结构化通道剪枝+INT8量化的组合策略对星上FPGA/NPU部署有参考价值；(4) focal-loss启发的类别平衡策略可借鉴到遥感场景中前景-背景极端不平衡(如小目标检测)问题；(5) Jetson Xavier NX也是遥感在轨/边缘部署的常见硬件平台，其实测FPS和加速比数据有参考意义。

Abstract: Real-time perception is a foundational requirement for advanced driver assistance systems (ADAS) and autonomous vehicles, yet embedded automotive platforms impose severe constraints on compute, memory, and power. This paper presents an optimized semantic segmentation architecture derived from the RetinaNet detection framework, adapted for dense pixel-wise prediction and tailored for deployment on resource-constrained embedded hardware. The proposed architecture, termed Opt-RetinaSeg, replaces the standard ResNet-50 backbone with a hybrid lightweight feature extractor, restructures the Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) to reduce redundant multi-scale computation, and introduces a compact segmentation head guided by focal-loss-inspired class balancing to address the severe foreground-background imbalance common in road scenes. We further apply a three-stage optimization pipeline consisting of structured channel pruning, post-training INT8 quantization, and knowledge distillation from a high-capacity teacher network. Evaluated on the Cityscapes and BDD100K datasets and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX and a Qualcomm QCS610 automotive SoC, the proposed model achieves 73.9% mIoU at 70.4 FPS, representing a 7.4x inference speedup and a 4x reduction in model size relative to the ResNet-50 baseline, with less than 3% accuracy degradation. These results indicate that RetinaNet-derived architectures, when systematically optimized, are viable candidates for real-time semantic segmentation in embedded automotive perception pipelines

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Experiments: 实验表明方法增强了与vision-language模型集成的儿童风险审查能力，生成更鲁棒的审查报告。未与遥感目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、加速或部署技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于AIGC视频内容安全领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不相关。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Artificial Intelligence-generated content (AIGC) is reshaping video production and circulation, exposing children to an increasing volume of AIGC videos. Unlike traditionally produced videos, AIGC videos often exhibit greater uncertainty in visual details, narrative coherence, and content expression, which may introduce developmentally inappropriate risks for children. However, existing video safety research is largely designed for general violation detection from an adult perspective and remains insufficient for identifying the fine-grained, implicit, and context-dependent risks that children may encounter when viewing AIGC videos. To address this gap, we study child-oriented AIGC video reviewing, making three contributions. First, we construct CAVSR, a benchmark of 605 real-world videos collected from multiple platforms, and develop a hierarchical risk taxonomy comprising 6 top-level categories and 26 fine-grained labels to support systematic evaluation of children's viewing risks. Second, we propose QVRS-E, a knowledge- and experience-augmented video reviewing framework that combines multi-agent collaboration with expert and experiential knowledge to support targeted evidence acquisition and fact-grounded reviewing decisions. Third, extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly enhances the reviewing of child-related risks integrated with vision-language models, and yields more robust review reports.

</details>


### [14] [Visual Token Compression Enhances Robustness of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22716)
*Shishen Gu,Jiequan Cui,Wenbo Hu,Zenglin Shi,Zhenzhen Hu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出通过视觉token剪枝来增强多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的鲁棒性，缓解越狱攻击和幻觉问题，同时附带降低推理成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的鲁棒性增强（防御越狱攻击和幻觉），并非遥感卫星目标检测或轻量化部署场景。虽然涉及"token pruning"（剪枝）和"inference cost reduction"（推理成本降低），但其动机、任务、数据集和评估指标均与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对MLLM鲁棒性有交叉兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型鲁棒性增强（防御越狱攻击、缓解幻觉）。应用场景：通用视觉-语言理解。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。摘要未说明具体数据集中的图像类型。

Model architecture: 基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM），具体架构摘要未说明。核心模块：通过测量视觉token与语言特征空间的距离，识别并迭代剪枝OOD（out-of-distribution）视觉token。

Lightweight method: 涉及视觉token剪枝（visual token pruning），但其主要动机是增强鲁棒性而非模型轻量化，推理成本降低是"side benefit"（附带收益）。不涉及剪枝（权重/通道剪枝）、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"reducing inference cost as a side benefit"，但无具体数据。

Datasets and metrics: 在7个多样化公开benchmark上评估，包括MME等通用多模态数据集。评估指标：越狱攻击防御提升13.29%（平均），幻觉缓解性能，以及通用数据集上的表现。不涉及遥感数据集（如DOTA, HRSC2016, DIOR, NWPU VHR-10等）。

Experiments: 在7个benchmark上验证，越狱防御平均提升13.29%，幻觉缓解表现一致，通用数据集（如MME）上保持强结果。摘要未说明与基线方法的详细对比和消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 轻量化仅作为附带收益，缺乏量化指标；3. 方法依赖多模态大语言模型架构，不适用于传统遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列、Faster R-CNN等）；4. 未讨论在边缘/星上部署的可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1）"token pruning"思想可类比于遥感目标检测中的特征图剪枝或注意力头剪枝，但需重新设计适用于遥感场景的剪枝准则；2）OOD检测思路可用于遥感在轨场景中剔除异常/噪声输入，但与本课题核心目标（轻量化目标检测）关联弱。

Abstract: In this paper, we show for the first time that visual token pruning enhances the robustness of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), mitigating vulnerabilities such as jailbreak attacks and hallucinations. Given that vision and language modalities cannot be perfectly aligned, the misaligned visual tokens might act as out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs, leading to unpredictable outputs and introducing potential vulnerabilities. Building on this insight, we aim to enhance model robustness against jailbreaks and hallucinations by reducing OOD visual tokens at robust-pruning layers, while also reducing inference cost as a side benefit. Specifically, we measure the distance between each visual token and the language feature space. Then, visual tokens with large distances are identified as OOD tokens, which can be iteratively pruned. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we evaluate it on seven diverse popular benchmarks. Notably, our method yields an average improvement of 13.29\% in defending jailbreak attacks, consistently achieves competitive performance in mitigating hallucinations, and maintains strong results on general datasets like MME.

</details>


### [15] [TOM-GS: Editable Video Representation via Temporal Opacity Modulation of Static 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22717)
*Marek Lisowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Kornel Howil,Piotr Biliński,Marcin Mazur,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TOM-GS，一种基于3D高斯泼溅的可编辑视频表示方法，通过连续时间不透明度公式实现静态3D空间组件的平滑淡入淡出，支持多种编辑操作。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视频表示与编辑（3D Gaussian Splatting + INR），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)，引入连续时间不透明度公式（continuous temporal opacity formulation），每个高斯体分配可学习的时间均值和尺度参数，实现静态3D空间组件的时序淡入淡出。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 在视觉保真度（visual fidelity）上优于先前的可编辑视频表示方法，且兼容现有3D编辑工具。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。论文本身未讨论计算效率、实时推理或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于3D视觉/视频编辑方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: While Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) and dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieve impressive results in video processing, they often fall short of producing representations that are easily editable. Recent methods address this by introducing complex spatial deformations or folded distributions, which constrain optimization and reduce flexibility for downstream editing. In this paper, we introduce TOM-GS, an editable video representation that forgoes complex deformations in favor of regular 3D Gaussians equipped with a continuous temporal opacity formulation. By assigning a learnable temporal mean and scale to the opacity of each Gaussian, our model enables static 3D spatial components to fade smoothly in and out of the scene. Grounded by robust, off-the-shelf pose estimation, our approach maintains a static spatial geometry that naturally supports a wide range of manual and physics-based edits. TOM-GS outperforms prior editable video representations in visual fidelity, while its reliance on standard 3D Gaussians ensures seamless compatibility with established 3D editing tools.

</details>


### [16] [DAMamba-UNet3D: A Parameter-Efficient Mamba State Space U-Net with Dynamic Adaptive Scan for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22718)
*Mohammad Arafat Hussain,Ellen Grant,Yangming Ou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于参数高效SSM（状态空间模型）的3D医学图像分割U-Net架构DAMamba-UNet3D，引入可学习的动态自适应扫描（DAS）替代固定扫描模式，在BraTS 2020上以约5.3M参数达到接近SegMamba（~70M）的分割性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦3D医学图像分割（MRI脑肿瘤），任务类型、数据模态（3D医学体积）、应用场景（临床诊断）均与遥感/卫星/Earth observation无关。虽然涉及轻量化设计（~5.3M参数）和SSM架构，但缺乏遥感场景验证和检测任务适配。

Reading priority: 2分。弱相关。SSM架构和参数高效设计思路有间接借鉴价值，但论文本身与遥感/卫星/目标检测/在轨部署均无直接关联。若研究方向关注SSM在遥感检测中的应用，可快速浏览其DAS机制和混合架构设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：3D医学图像分割（语义分割）；场景：临床脑肿瘤分割；传感器：MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：脑肿瘤区域（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 混合编码器-解码器U-Net：编码器阶段E2-E4集成三平面3D-DAS（动态自适应扫描）块，其余部分保留卷积。包含两种变体：DAMamba-UNet3D（~5.3M参数）和DAMamba-L（~70M参数，宽DAS原生变体）。

Lightweight method: 参数高效设计：通过混合架构（卷积+SSM）而非纯SSM/Transformer实现轻量化，~5.3M参数远低于SegMamba的~70M。但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明部署相关信息（延迟、FLOPs、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘/星上部署）。仅报告参数量（~5.3M和~70M），无推理速度或硬件适配信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BraTS 2020（脑肿瘤分割）；评价指标：mean Dice（全体积逐例评估）；实验设置：五折交叉验证。

Experiments: DAMamba-UNet3D（~5.3M）mean Dice 0.815±0.013，参数成本约为SegMamba（0.824±0.014，~70M）的1/13。DAMamba-L（~70M）达到0.829±0.012，超过SegMamba 0.5pt。消融实验表明编码器仅DAS放置是关键，瓶颈和解码器SSM块会降低Dice。

Limitations: 1. 仅验证于医学图像（BraTS 2020），未在遥感数据上测试；2. 任务为分割而非目标检测；3. 无推理速度、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标；4. 未讨论在轨/边缘部署可行性；5. 动态自适应扫描的计算开销未量化。

Ideas for my research: 1. SSM（Mamba）在遥感目标检测中的潜力：O(n)全局传播效率可能优于Transformer的O(n²)，适合遥感大尺寸图像；2. 混合架构思路（卷积+SSM）可借鉴到遥感检测轻量化；3. 动态自适应扫描（DAS）思想可迁移到遥感旋转框检测中的特征排序/扫描策略；4. 参数高效设计（~5.3M）表明SSM可大幅压缩模型规模，对星上部署有参考价值。但需注意：本文是3D医学分割，迁移到遥感2D检测需重新设计。

Abstract: We propose parameter-efficient SSM-based U-Net architectures for 3D medical image segmentation. Convolutional U-Nets afford O(n) local mixing per layer but lack explicit global context; transformers provide global reasoning at O(n^2) cost in sequence length $n$. State-space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, offer $O(n)$ global propagation per block. Yet, existing medical SSM segmenters rely on fixed scan patterns and large parameter budgets. Dynamic Adaptive Scan (DAS), which learns data-dependent reordering before selective scan, has not been applied to medical imaging or extended to 3D volumes. We propose DAMamba-UNet3D, a hybrid encoder-decoder that integrates tri-plane 3D-DAS blocks at encoder stages E2-E4 while retaining convolutions elsewhere (~5.3M parameters). On BraTS 2020 five-fold cross-validation, DAMamba-UNet3D achieves mean Dice 0.815+/-0.013 (full-volume per-case evaluation) at ~13x lower parameter cost than SegMamba (0.824+\-0.014, ~70M). At comparable scale, DAMamba-L (~70M), a wide DAS-native variant with encoder-only DAMamba and a convolutional bottleneck, reaches 0.829+\-0.012, surpassing retrained SegMamba by 0.5pt. Component ablations show that encoder-only DAS placement is critical as bottleneck and decoder SSM blocks lower Dice. Together, the results suggest that learned tri-plane DAS in a hybrid U-Net is competitive with, and under our large-scale design may improve upon, SegMamba's fixed Tri-orientated Mamba (ToM) scanning on BraTS 2020. Code: https://github.com/marafathussain/DAMamba-UNet3D.

</details>


### [17] [$γ$-Bridge: A Look-Parametric Diffusion Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22719)
*Xuran Hu,Yujie Zhu,Tengxi Wang,Jilong Li,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理参数（视数L）的Gamma桥扩散模型γ-Bridge，用于SAR图像去斑，通过将桥过程时间直接映射为物理视数，实现单模型在多种SAR传感器上的零样本去斑。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文涉及SAR（合成孔径雷达，遥感核心传感器之一），但任务是图像去斑（despeckling），而非目标检测。不涉及任何目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署相关内容。仅因SAR传感器与遥感卫星相关，对遥感图像预处理有潜在价值，但与"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分 — 与课题方向弱相关。虽然涉及SAR遥感，但任务是去斑而非目标检测，且不涉及任何轻量化/在轨部署内容。如果课题涉及SAR目标检测的前处理去噪，可参考；否则阅读优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：SAR图像去斑（despeckling），属于图像复原/增强任务。传感器：SAR（合成孔径雷达），涉及6种星载和机载SAR传感器。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无目标类别。场景：遥感SAR图像预处理。

Model architecture: 基于扩散桥（bridge）的生成模型，核心是Gamma桥过程，前向过程由物理视数L(t)参数化，而非传统扩散模型的信噪比调度。包含Gamma-Lévy反向过程（支持随机和确定性两种采样），以及两步一致性损失（two-step consistency loss）稳定低SNR单视情况下的多步推理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。扩散模型通常计算开销大，在轨部署可行性低。

Datasets and metrics: 训练：在自然图像上添加合成Gamma噪声（仅L_obs=1训练）。测试：6种星载和机载SAR传感器数据（传感器类型未具体列出）。评价指标：在标准合成基准上达到领先结果，具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在6种不同SAR传感器上进行了零样本测试，无需传感器特定微调。在标准合成基准上取得领先结果。与现有去斑扩散模型对比，优势在于物理可解释的输入输出控制。

Limitations: 1. 与目标检测完全无关，不涉及检测头、锚框、NMS等检测组件。2. 扩散模型计算量大，不满足在轨轻量化需求。3. 仅针对SAR去斑预处理任务，不涉及检测模型本身。4. 摘要未报告推理速度、模型大小等工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该工作的物理参数化思路（将扩散过程时间映射为物理可解释参数）可借鉴到遥感目标检测中的数据增强或域适应中。2. SAR去斑可作为遥感目标检测的前处理步骤，但需评估其对检测精度的影响。3. 该工作展示了单模型多传感器泛化的能力，这种"零样本泛化"思路对遥感检测模型跨传感器迁移有启发。4. 整体而言，对"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Multiplicative Gamma noise is a signal-dependent degradation in coherent imaging; synthetic aperture radar (SAR) despeckling is its most prominent real-world instance. Existing diffusion denoisers parameterize their forward process by abstract signal-to-noise schedules rather than by the physical look number $L$, so different deployment scenarios typically require separately trained models, and transfer from synthetic Gamma training to real SAR remains challenging without clean ground truth. We introduce $γ$-Bridge, a look-parametric bridge whose schedule $L(t)$ connects the noisy observation at $L_{obs}$ to the clean limit through exact multiplicative Gamma marginals. Its closed-form Gamma--Lévy reverse posterior admits both stochastic and deterministic processes, while observation conditioning and a two-step consistency loss stabilize multi-step inference in the low-SNR single-look regime. Because bridge time directly represents $L$, one conditioned network can smart-start from any admissible input look and stop at a target look number. These two orthogonal controls enable zero-shot restoration over the full admissible grid after training only at $L_{obs} = 1$ on natural images with synthetic Gamma corruption. Combined with a homogeneous-patch look estimator, $γ$-Bridge processes data from six spaceborne and airborne SAR sensors without sensor-specific fine-tuning, achieving leading results on standard synthetic benchmarks while providing physically interpretable input and output controls absent from prior denoisers. Codes are released \href{https://github.com/Teriri1999/GammaBridge}{here}.

</details>


### [18] [An Interactive Vision Language Platform for Cognitive Remediation in Schizophrenia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22721)
*Nassira Ait Mehdi,Milissa Temmam,Slimane Larabi*

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型的自动化框架，用于精神分裂症患者认知康复任务中的动作验证，通过摄像头监控桌面场景并分析患者操作玩具车辆的行为。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文涉及的是医疗康复领域的认知任务动作验证，使用视觉语言模型分析桌面场景中的玩具车辆操作，完全不涉及遥感卫星、地球观测、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：认知康复任务中的动作验证；应用场景：精神分裂症患者的临床康复治疗；传感器类型：普通摄像头（监控桌面场景）；目标类别：玩具车辆、道路、环岛、公园等微型场景元素。与遥感目标检测无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该框架面向临床环境，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建数据集，包含4634个桌面认知康复视频场景。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标。

Experiments: 实验结果表明该框架能有效桥接底层物理遥测与高层临床反馈，提供可扩展且客观的认知康复解决方案。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关技术或部署优化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医疗康复领域的视觉语言模型应用，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何与本研究课题相关的内容。

Abstract: Cognitive remediation tasks often require patients to perform structured actions involving object manipulation and sequential reasoning. For patients diagnosed with schizophrenia, these tasks are crucial for addressing severe cognitive deficits. However, evaluating the correctness of these physical actions generally relies on manual observation by clinicians, which introduces subjectivity and limits the scalability of therapeutic interventions. In this paper, we propose an automated framework based on Vision-Language Models for action verification in cognitive remediation tasks tailored for schizophrenia rehabilitation. The proposed system relies on a camera-monitored tabletop environment composed of structured miniature scenes including roads, a roundabout, a park, and toy vehicles. Patients receive audio instructions describing goal-oriented spatial actions to perform by manipulating a toy vehicle. These interactive physical activities are specifically designed to stimulate targeted cognitive functions, such as sustained attention, motor coordination, spatial navigation, and cognitive flexibility. To verify the correctness of the performed actions without requiring continuous clinical oversight, the system analyzes the video feed tracking the patient's hand and toy movements. A fine-tuned Vision-Language Model interprets the recorded video sequences and generates semantic descriptions of the observed activities, enabling high-level verification of the executed actions with respect to the initial textual instructions. A dedicated dataset of 4634 tabletop cognitive remediation video scenarios was collected to evaluate the proposed approach. Experimental results demonstrate that our specialized framework effectively bridges low-level physical telemetry with high-level clinical feedback, presenting a scalable and objective solution for advanced cognitive rehabilitation.

</details>


### [19] [A New Kind of Adversarial Example: Measuring the Human-Model Gap, and Its Relationship to OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22722)
*Ali Borji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究一种"大扰动但模型仍正确预测"的对抗攻击，发现人类识别率大幅下降而模型保持100%准确率，且现有OOD检测和防御手段均失效。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是对抗攻击的一个特殊变体（大扰动下模型仍正确预测），实验在MNIST、CIFAR-10、ImageNet等通用视觉数据集上进行，不涉及遥感图像、卫星传感器、在轨部署或模型轻量化任何要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：对抗攻击（adversarial attack）的变体研究；应用场景：通用图像分类；传感器类型：无（通用RGB图像）；目标类别：手写数字、通用物体（CIFAR-10/ImageNet类别）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及使用了一个"independent recognizer proxy"和CLIP zero-shot proxy进行评估，以及使用了Mahalanobis距离检测器进行特征空间分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST、CIFAR-10、ImageNet（均为通用视觉数据集，非遥感数据集）。评价指标：模型准确率（100%）、人类识别率（~49% on CIFAR-10）、OOD检测率（0% for confidence/energy-based, 100% for Mahalanobis）、校准误差ECE（~0）、鲁棒准确率（45% for adversarial training）。

Experiments: 主要发现：(i) 人类识别率降至~49%而模型保持100%；(ii) 基于置信度和能量的OOD检测器完全失效（0%检测率），Mahalanobis检测器可检测100%但被自适应攻击绕过；(iii) 对抗训练等经典防御无法降低攻击成功率。机制分析表明攻击破坏低频纹理远快于边缘/形状结构。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，因此局限性不具参考价值。从自身领域看，实验仅在小型通用数据集上进行，未在遥感等实际应用场景验证。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是对抗攻击的极端变体，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。唯一可能的间接启发是：在星上部署轻量化模型时，需要考虑对抗鲁棒性，但本文研究的是"大扰动下模型仍正确"这一特殊现象，与常规对抗攻击防御方向不同。

Abstract: Almost all adversarial attacks add an imperceptible perturbation to fool a model. We instead study the opposite: a large, clearly visible perturbation that causes the model to keep its original, correct prediction, even though a human would no longer recognize the image. Prior work showed such examples can be generated at scale but left three questions untested: whether humans really perform worse than the model, whether standard out-of-distribution (OOD) detection and calibration tools catch it, and whether existing defenses mitigate it. We answer all three on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ImageNet. (i) An independent recognizer proxy drops to ~49% on CIFAR-10 while the model stays at 100% -- a gap a small human pilot (N=5) corroborates directly and that is not explained by signal loss (a matched-magnitude Gaussian control degrades recognizability faster); a CLIP zero-shot proxy confirms the gap at ImageNet scale too. (ii) Confidence- and energy-based OOD detectors and calibration are structurally blind (0% detection, ECE ~= 0), while a feature-space Mahalanobis detector flags 100% -- but is evaded by an adaptive attacker at no cost to success. (iii) No classical defense, including adversarial training (45% robust accuracy), reduces attack success (correlation with large-epsilon_l resistance r ~= 0). A mechanistic analysis further shows the attack destroys low-level texture far faster than edge/shape structure.

</details>

### [20] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C-VCE，一种将分类器内置到扩散模型概念瓶颈层中的视觉反事实解释框架，用于生成最小编辑的图像反事实样本。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于视觉反事实解释（visual counterfactual explanations）和扩散模型的可解释性，属于可解释AI/生成模型方向，应用场景为医学等安全关键领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何轻量化部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉反事实解释（visual counterfactual explanations）；场景：安全关键领域（如医学）；传感器类型：自然图像（CelebA人脸数据集）；目标类别：人脸属性（语义概念）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于扩散模型（diffusion-based），引入概念瓶颈层（concept bottleneck layer）将分类器内置到生成模型中。包含概率正则化器（probabilistic regularizer）和梯度掩码（gradient-based mask）用于控制编辑区域。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CelebA；评价指标：flip rates（翻转率）、视觉接近度（visually closer to the input）、失真度（less distorted）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 在CelebA上，C-VCE匹配或提升了翻转率，同时生成的反事实图像在视觉上更接近输入、失真更小，优于依赖独立噪声鲁棒分类器的基线方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅在人脸数据集上验证，未在遥感场景下测试；3. 扩散模型推理速度慢，不适合在轨实时部署；4. 未讨论模型大小或计算效率。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：概念瓶颈层（concept bottleneck）的思想或许可用于遥感目标检测中的可解释性分析，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化、实时推理、星上部署）无关。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>

Experiments: 展示了零样本排列稳定性（zero-shot permutation stability），在传感器打乱后精度几乎无下降；单个预训练模型可同时完成数据补全、预测和虚拟传感。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；未涉及任何视觉/图像任务；未讨论模型效率或部署约束。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>

Experiments: QDockBank基准上，中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%的初始无效案例被恢复；最强控制器在87%的序列上改善了cycle-3能量，同时保持96%的Ramachandran-favored几何。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对5残基短片段，扩展到更长蛋白质链的可行性未讨论。2. 使用Qiskit Aer噪声模拟而非真实量子硬件。3. 与遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>

Model architecture: 核心模型：JSL Medical Small 7B v2（小型医疗推理LLM）；辅助组件：RAG（检索增强生成）；管线结构：五阶段工作流（结构化临床提取→约束检索→受限候选生成→证据三角验证→可审计最终决策）。

Lightweight method: 论文的核心轻量化思路是"用小模型+工作流约束替代大模型"，即通过精心设计的推理管线（pipeline/harnessing）来弥补小模型（7B参数）的能力不足，而非传统的剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。该方法在医疗领域有效，但方法论本身与遥感轻量化目标检测的常用技术路线差异较大。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。给出了成本指标：每例0.0072美元（24K tokens on A100），延迟24秒。参数量7B，远超出星上部署的典型资源约束（通常需<100M参数级别）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DiagnosisArena（915例病例）；评价指标：top-1 diagnostic accuracy（60.14%）；对比基线：Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro等。

Experiments: 主要结果：DeepLens Diagnosis Agent在915例基准上top-1准确率60.14%，为小/中规模模型中最高；无工作流时仅23.99%（+36点提升）；成本比Claude Sonnet 4.5低35-45%，准确率高出9.70pp和9.17pp。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。7B参数规模不适合星上部署。医疗诊断的"多阶段推理管线"思路与遥感目标检测的"单阶段/两阶段检测器+轻量化"范式差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：（1）"用小模型+结构化工作流弥补能力差距"的思路，可类比为"用轻量检测器+后处理优化弥补精度损失"；（2）多阶段管线中的"证据三角验证"概念，可类比遥感检测中的多视角/多时相验证策略；（3）可审计的中间产物输出，对星上可解释性有启发。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [25] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIITA方法，通过存储校正方向原型并在推理时进行门控隐藏状态适配，解决小语言模型在资源受限场景下的持续学习灾难性遗忘问题。

Task and scene: 任务类型：持续学习(Continual Learning)；应用场景：小语言模型在资源受限设备上的持续适应；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MIITA框架：存储监督经验为紧凑的校正方向原型(correction-direction prototypes)与语义锚点(semantic anchors)，推理时通过语义和不确定性线索检索，通过门控临时隐藏状态适配(gated temporary hidden-state adaptation)实现非破坏性知识复用。无需主干网络更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及"多种监督持续学习设置(diverse supervised CL settings)"和"最终性能(final performance)与遗忘缓解(forgetting mitigation)"。

Experiments: 摘要未给出具体实验结果数值。仅称MIITA在多种持续学习设置下持续改善最终性能并缓解遗忘。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化核心技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；4. 无部署相关量化指标；5. 方法针对语言模型设计，难以直接迁移到视觉检测模型。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：1. "推理时适配(inference-time adaptation)"的概念——在轨场景中，遥感模型可能需要在推理时根据当前轨道/光照/传感器状态进行轻量级适配，MIITA的门控隐藏状态适配思路可启发"无需重训的星上模型适配"；2. "紧凑原型存储"思想——在星上存储资源受限时，可用类似方式存储少量代表性样本或校正向量。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为领域差异大。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>

Experiments: 实验表明程序化评判器可匹配13B规模LLM评判器的性能；PAJAMA系统在准确率和吞吐量上均有所提升，推进了Pareto前沿；在RewardBench上，从程序评判蒸馏出的奖励模型优于从专有LLM标签训练的模型，API成本降低两个数量级。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。论文本身未讨论遥感或目标检测问题。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [27] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向LLM智能体的策略性遗忘框架(SF-AMS)，通过效用驱动的记忆生存机制和复合重要性评分来管理长上下文依赖，提升多步推理能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)智能体的长上下文记忆管理问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>

*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，通过将每个特征图视为一个臂，在固定预算内评估移除后的损失变化并排序，实现结构化剪枝。

Reading priority: 2分——作为剪枝方法参考可读，但与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向直接相关性低。建议仅作为剪枝技术储备，优先级不高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：结构化特征图剪枝（feature-map pruning）；场景：通用图像分类（非遥感）；传感器类型：无；目标类别：MNIST数字、CIFAR-10/100物体、SVHN街景数字、CUB-200鸟类、Oxford Flowers花卉——均为通用视觉分类数据集，非遥感目标检测场景。

Model architecture: 基础模型：LeNet（MNIST实验），以及扩展至其他CNN架构（摘要未明确说明扩展模型名称）；剪枝对象：卷积层的完整输出通道（特征图）及其对应的滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核。

Lightweight method: 结构化特征图剪枝（structured feature-map pruning），基于多臂老虎机（Multi-Armed Bandits, MAB）框架，使用UCB1和Thompson Sampling两种策略。方法特点：损失感知（loss-aware）、逐次评估（temporarily mask + evaluate loss change）、安全移除奖励（safe-removal reward）。非剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等其它轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提及减少"storage and inference cost"和"convolutional computation"，但未给出具体数值。未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011, Oxford Flowers 102；评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy），以及剪枝后与未剪枝模型的准确率对比；统计检验：Friedman检验和Nemenyi检验。

Experiments: 对比方法：UCB1 vs Thompson Sampling vs 直接评估（direct/oracle-style）vs 贪婪剪枝（greedy）vs 基于幅值剪枝（magnitude-based pruning）；基础实验：LeNet/MNIST验证；扩展实验：6个数据集上的分类任务；结果：UCB1获得最高平均秩次，Thompson Sampling次之，两者均显著优于贪婪和幅值剪枝，与未剪枝模型统计上无显著差异。

Limitations: 1. 仅在图像分类任务上验证，未涉及目标检测（尤其是遥感旋转框/小目标检测）；2. 未在遥感数据集（如DOTA, HRSC2016, DIOR, NWPU VHR-10等）上实验；3. 未报告FLOPs、参数量缩减比例、推理速度等关键轻量化指标；4. 未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性；5. 剪枝策略的评估开销（每次play需前向推理一次）可能影响实际加速比。

Ideas for my research: 1. 可将MAB剪枝框架迁移到遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列、RetinaNet等）的特征图剪枝中，替代传统的幅值/BN剪枝；2. 损失感知的剪枝策略比贪婪/幅值方法更优，可尝试用于遥感小目标检测模型的通道剪枝；3. UCB1策略简单有效，可嵌入到遥感模型轻量化流程中作为剪枝决策模块；4. 需补充FLOPs、参数量、推理延迟等指标，并验证在遥感场景（如舰船/飞机检测）上的有效性。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency. Yet on-device inference faces a fundamental constraint: high energy consumption on battery-powered, resource-limited hardware. While model compression and runtime acceleration have been widely studied, the effect of \emph{prompt design} on energy efficiency remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical study of the relationship between prompt wording and energy consumption for on-device LLMs. Using real power measurements collected on a smartphone, we quantify how linguistic features, particularly imperative keywords and instruction structure, affect decoding length and total energy. Our results show consistent energy differences across verbs and tasks, indicating that prompt engineering is a lightweight lever for improving energy efficiency.

</details>


### [33] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星相关任务。任务为编码代理的安全测试，场景为系统运维中的红队测试。

Experiments: 在多个agent框架和模型骨干上验证，将不安全操作伪装为单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现、验证等常规任务后，不安全执行成功率显著提升。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI安全领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩加速。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers. These results show that coding agents in system operations remain insecure under task disguise: once risky intent is hidden inside plausible engineering tasks, the agent can be induced to carry out unsafe actions on the surrounding system. More broadly, coding agents in system operations still demand stronger security testing and safeguards.

</details>
